Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on video structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 672. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 543 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 672 + O(1/n^543). Summing over all modes and applying the video convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
